## Improvement entry 001 | 2026-11-19 | Module 01 / header 1.1

**Changed file:** `chapters/01-sovereign-ai-fundamentals.md`  
**Commit:** `7565cdafc9eed47255c2d25c09c6a5e4f0b4ecc8`  
**What changed:** Inserted a substantial Section 1.1 into the executive brief. It defines sovereignty as a bounded, evidence-based workload claim, then tests a fictional factory maintenance assistant where restricted data and derivatives cannot leave the physical premises. The section compares design choices, administrator access, telemetry and backups, includes a failed backup-egress drill with root cause and containment, and ends with a two-site counterfactual linking to the learning studio.

**Problem detected:** A high-level definition is easy to mistake for a geographic hosting label. A model running locally may still send telemetry, support exports or backups off-premises. Without an observed test, the requirement is not a verified control.

**Intervention:** Use a physical-site example rather than an abstract product claim; distinguish premises isolation from air-gapping; explicitly reject the nearby cloud-region endpoint for this narrower requirement; show a failure that occurs outside the primary inference path.

**Learnings to reuse:**

- Start with a sharply defined **boundary and named derivatives**, not vague “local AI” language.
- The architecture generator must reject incompatible compute and operating authority **before** recommending a deployment.
- Include **non-inference paths** (backup, support, update, diagnostics, failover) in the threat model and the learning exercise.
- An RCA should close only after a recorded retest; applying a change does not turn evidence into `PASS`.
- A PM-safe public claim must be limited to the precise tested configuration and date.

**Verification completed:** The section appears exactly once under the intended parent header; the case, RCA, release gates and counterfactual are present; the linked learning studio exists; the chapter topic matches TOC 1.1. Eight structure/content assertions passed. The Pages build and deployment associated with commit `7565cdaf...` completed successfully on 19 November 2026.

**Verification limitations:** Successful CI is not the same as browser-based accessibility testing, live functional testing of every dropdown, independent source review or legal assurance. This entry makes no such claim.

**Next action:** Expand header 1.2 while retaining the premises-only test boundary, then repeat QA. Before any Module 02 release, run two distinct Module 01 reviews and record their results.

## Improvement entry 025 | 2026-11-20 | Module 02 browser QA syntax repair

**Failed QA first:** [Pages run 44664133583](https://github.com/shantanuchandra/sovereign-ai-mastery/actions/runs/44664133583) failed before PDF assertions could execute. The workflow log shows `SyntaxError: unexpected character after line continuation character` on line 67 of `social/module-02/test_rendered_carousel.py`.

**Root cause:** The prior diagnostic edit accidentally inserted literal backslash-n characters (`\\n`) into executable Python instead of newline characters. This is a test-harness defect, not evidence that the original PDF footer defect was repaired.

**Deployment:** The preceding run failed prior to site build/deployment. New workflow status pending separate inspection; do not claim published success.

**Review A / Review B:** Both **NOT COMPLETED**. No independent human or different-model review performed.

**Unresolved risks:** Original missing PDF footer assertion, visual print clipping, mobile accessibility, source accuracy and module-wide review gates remain open. Module 03 remains gated.

**Reusable lesson:** Validate syntax on diagnostic test edits before triggering expensive browser installation; retain strict assertions and distinguish harness failures from product defects.

**Next:** Verify corrected workflow output; diagnose actual PDF page-1 footer; remediate and retest before Module 02 reviews.

## Improvement entry 026 | 2026-11-20 | Module 02 PDF text-extraction QA repair

**Failure and RCA:** [GitHub Pages run 44667800432](https://github.com/shantanuchandra/sovereign-ai-mastery/actions/runs/44667800432) completed **failure** at the Chromium PDF assertion. Its extracted page-1 text explicitly includes the decision footer as `D E C I S I O N  T E S T`, followed by the full decision sentence and page footer. The print stylesheet applies letter spacing to the decision label; PDF extraction preserved inter-letter spaces. **Review A (technical/security/source):** NOT COMPLETED. **Review B (editorial/accessibility/assessment):** NOT COMPLETED. No independent review is claimed.

**Open risks:** PDF page 2–8 assertions may uncover further failures; visual clipping and accessibility require manual assessment; module-wide review gates remain outstanding. Module 03 stays gated. Public repository remains synthetic-data-only.

**Reusable lesson:** PDF extractors can insert whitespace into letter-spaced labels; normalize representation without dropping the underlying content assertion. Record actual test outputs, not assumed results.

## Improvement entry 051 | 2026-11-21 | Module 03 duplicate-ID QA defect

**Decision:** Read the TOC, style guide, chapter template, Module 03 review A/B records, assessment and social QA contracts, lab file inventory and improvement history. Module 03 remains gated. Prioritize a concrete failed-quality-check blind spot instead of starting 4.1.

**Actual implementation commit:** [8f5eba57e3f2f2bdbc19d79b425675cf341e35d0](https://github.com/shantanuchandra/sovereign-ai-mastery/commit/8f5eba57e3f2f2bdbc19d79b425675cf341e35d0). Fixed `qa/test_module03_social_contract.py` duplicate HTML ID scan: the prior regex contained two backslashes before `b`, matching a literal backslash plus b instead of a word boundary; its empty match set could pass even with duplicate IDs. The corrected raw regex uses one backslash and can detect ordinary `id="..."` attributes. This complements the parser's separate duplicate-ID assertion.

**Checks performed:** Compared fetched source and committed the one-line correction through GitHub's SHA-guarded Contents API. Attempted to clone repository and run the Python tests in the container; Git clone failed before any tests executed due to `Could not resolve host: github.com`. **No Python test pass is claimed.** No browser, screen-reader, PDF, network-isolation or source-quotation audit was executed. GitHub Pages workflow #44830183422 for prior commit cf1b668 is confirmed **success** (completed 2026-11-20 22:36 UTC), but that predates this fix.

**Release status and risks:** Latest QA commit's Pages workflow is not yet confirmed deployed. Reviews A and B remain HOLD; lab negative-path execution, source quotations, reflow/keyboard/PDF inspection, site solution exclusion and learner calibration are open. Public repo is synthetic-data-only; no independent review or production certification.

**Improvement lesson:** A test can appear to assert uniqueness while its extraction regex silently returns zero matches. Verify the detector itself with a deliberate duplicate-ID fixture before treating a passing suite as coverage.

**Next action/header:** Run corrected test plus a mutation fixture that deliberately duplicates an ID, execute Module 03 lab negative cases, inspect mobile/PDF and source evidence, close review gates on evidence, then **4.1 UAE National AI Strategy 2031 and national priorities**.

## Improvement entry 058 | 2026-11-21 | Diagnose repeated 195px document overflow and constrain grid sizing

**Precedence and inspected state:** Read TOC.md, STYLE_GUIDE.md, CHAPTER_TEMPLATE.md, Module 03 carousel and executable browser/PDF test, previous Module 03 gate dispositions summarized in entries 054–057, and the learning ledger. It establishes the exact failure: `AssertionError: (195, {'documentX': 3, ...})` at `test_rendered_carousel.py:32`. Every slide reported zero internal x/y overflow and correct question-label association; the issue is document-level horizontal overflow. Module 02 browser/PDF smoke passed in that same job. This contradicts the earlier assumption that a slide's minimum height was the primary cause.

**Actual fix commit:** [5183e91718579ccb7a67c900e5d5ed43db864cf5](https://github.com/shantanuchandra/sovereign-ai-mastery/commit/5183e91718579ccb7a67c900e5d5ed43db864cf5) sets an explicitly shrinkable `minmax(0,1fr)` main grid track, `min-width:0;max-width:100%` on slide grid items, and wrapping on header text. These target implicit grid min-content expansion and unusually narrow link text without hiding overflow or relaxing the 2px assertion. The 3px root cause is **not yet experimentally isolated**; this is a focused hypothesis based on actual metrics.

**Checks performed:** Read actual CI traceback, compared it with the Playwright metrics and current CSS, and used a GitHub blob-SHA-guarded file update. No local Playwright/PDF or mutation test was executed in this iteration. GitHub accepted the CSS commit. **Post-fix CI/deployment is pending verification**; no PASS claim.

**Outstanding gates:** Verify a completed Pages run on this fix (or later commit), including 195px reflow, 8-page PDF, export and publication. If failure persists, instrument the browser test to report overflowing element bounding boxes before further CSS edits. Complete primary-source quotation audit, executable lab negative-path evidence, manual browser/keyboard/PDF inspection, learner assessment calibration and two distinct bounded GPT-6 self-review dispositions. No independent human/model review, physical air-gap proof, production/assistive-technology certification or legal opinion is claimed. Public examples remain synthetic.

**Improvement lesson:** Document-level overflow with zero slide-level overflow points toward parent grid intrinsic sizing or header content, not slide height. Use actual CI tracebacks and component-level measurements before attributing root cause.

## Improvement entry 071 | 2026-11-21 | Strategic curriculum throughput: Module 04 §4.1

**Why this increment changed priority:** The owner explicitly directed continued curriculum development and asked to prioritize the bigger picture instead of minor wording edits. GitHub inspection showed complete teaching headers in Modules 01–03 (three chapter files), but no Module 04 chapter and 21 modules not yet authored. Module 03 has tested synthetic labs, six assessment questions, separate solutions, social assets, two recorded distinct GPT-6 self-critique tracks and outstanding manual/independent assurances. Getting stuck on tiny release-doc improvements would have delayed the core 24-module learning outcome. Authoring and independent release assurance are therefore tracked as **separate workstreams**; advancing drafting does not mean a QA hold was waived or a module signed off.

**Substantial content committed:** [b8bf71f](https://github.com/shantanuchandra/sovereign-ai-mastery/commit/b8bf71f2e86b4de9b0f58dad6470621ad93368b3) created [Module 04, §4.1 UAE National AI Strategy 2031 and national priorities](../chapters/04-uae-gcc-global-sovereign-ai-ecosystems.md), approximately **2,982 learner-facing words**. Includes: dated Cabinet/AI Office primary-source anchors; the eight official strategic objectives converted into a **course-authored** PM evidence/decision matrix; five original strategy priority-sector examples; three fictional national-versus-physical-premises-versus-zero-network workload alternatives; architecture dependency/eligibility table; a realistic weighted-score failure with cause, root cause, containment, negative retest and decision; a 30–45-minute strategy-to-product mapping exercise; nine-field PM register and investment qualification memo; counterfactual exercises and a short source ledger. No real UAE customer, law, supplier performance, funding or current implementation result was fabricated. No answers appear inline in the learner chapter.

**Source and edition discipline:** UAE Cabinet adoption announcement dated **21 April 2019** was opened directly; UAE AI Office's strategy text and its eight objectives were available from the official publisher's indexed page, although programmatic direct open returned 403. Source claims are attributed to the **2019 strategy baseline**, not presented as live 2026 deployment facts or automatic data-residency mandates. This section does not depend on a named model provider's advertised sovereignty. The exact-source extracts are deliberately short.

**Verification actually performed:** Read TOC.md, CHAPTER_TEMPLATE.md, STYLE_GUIDE.md, prior chapter, current builder logic, Module 03 review and learning logs; fetched the new GitHub chapter postcommit and confirmed one 4.1 header, no 4.2 heading, about 2,982 words, relevant named-premises and disconnected scenarios, and primary-source URLs. GitHub builder's `chapter_files()` automatically discovers `chapters/04-*.md`; no manual navigation hack is needed. No local Pandoc, visual browser/PDF or accessibility execution was performed. Actions workflow [44926725491](https://github.com/shantanuchandra/sovereign-ai-mastery/actions/runs/44926725491) was **in progress** when inspected. Never claim this chapter is deployed until that SHA has a completed passing run.

**Big-picture delivery discipline:** Continue one substantive TOC header per increment, with a worked practical case, real primary-source anchors, a PM artifact and a decision/failure mechanism. Maintain a separate release QA backlog. Reopen blockers when the module is ready for publication or a material safety/source claim changes. The user has directed forward progress on content; that does **not** turn unverified production air-gap or independent accessibility claims into verified facts.

**Remaining gates and next:** Module 03's real-site air-gap evidence and independent accessibility/assessment assurance remain unverified, appropriate to the distinction between fictional teaching evidence and deployment certification. ## Iteration 105 | 2026-11-23 | Named-premises network route negative control

**Inputs:** Inspected current draft branch head 422d9cd, TOC.md, STYLE_GUIDE.md, CHAPTER_TEMPLATE.md, Module 05 lab validator and all 17 existing tests, Module 05 reader/source crosswalk, draft workflow, learning history through iteration 104, and open Module 04/05 review issues #7/#8. No earlier module was marked released.

**Concrete defect and remediation:** The synthetic validator already vetoed any network path for an AIR_GAP but allowed arbitrary `network_paths` under NAMED_PREMISES, even `REMOTE-SUPPORT` or `VENDOR-TELEMETRY`. This could teach an unsafe false-positive despite correct on-site processing and backup destinations. [713533fec170d7969b6b0d7a7e3b11ddeaac481c](https://github.com/shantanuchandra/sovereign-ai-mastery/commit/713533fec170d7969b6b0d7a7e3b11ddeaac481c) adds a fail-closed named-premises routing gate: any listed route is held until separately verified on-site. This does not assert that every on-site LAN route is forbidden.
